# Agents · P · part 5

- **plan-reviewer**: Use this agent to audit a plan file (typically under ~/.claude/plans/) from a senior staff / chief-architect perspective before the main agent calls ExitPlanMode. - **plan-reviewer**: プランナーが立てた実装計画を独立コンテキストでレビューする独立ペルソナ。entry ゲート「plan-review」（reads: plan）から起動される。Anthropic の harness 設計記事における Evaluator が Generator の作業に着手前承認を出す仕組み（Sprint contracts）に相当する。 ([m-kuwata/claude-harness](https://github.com/m-kuwata/claude-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reviewer**: Reviews an approved Reef plan for semantic drift between its artifacts — invariants, brief, ADRs, tasks, config. Fresh context, citation-required, read-only judge. TO THE CALLER: a contradiction between two SOURCES OF TRUTH (a task file vs an ADR) is a HALT, never an auto-fix — you do not have the standing to pick the winner. - **plan-reviewer**: Iterative plan-review loop (Codex reviews, Claude adjudicates) on tasks/builds/&lt;slug&gt;/plan.md against its governing spec; plan/spec drift is the primary hunt target.
